		FRA = {

			}

			}
			DSW_FRA_refresh_party_leader = yes
		}

		complete_national_focus = FRA_The_Opening_Volley
		complete_national_focus = FRA_rassemblent_national
		complete_national_focus = FRA_immigration_regulation

country_event = {
	trigger = {
		FRA = {
			has_country_flag = DSW_FRA_tfr_active
		}
	}
	id = france.998
	title = france.998.t
	desc = france.998.d
	picture = GFX_Mr_Domino
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = france.998.a
		set_cosmetic_tag = SER_yugo_euro
		set_variable = {
			ruling_party = 20
		}
		set_variable = {
			DSW_FRA_tfr_party = 20
		}
		set_variable = {
			DSW_FRA_scripted_elections = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			disable_elections = 1
		}
		clear_array = gov_coalition_array
		set_country_flag = DSW_FRA_tfr_active
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = nationalist
			elections_allowed = no
			last_election = "2022.04.10"
			election_frequency = 60
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			DSW_FRA_refresh_party_leader = yes
		}
		KOS = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_character = DSW_TFR_KOS_mr_domino_char
					}
				}
				recruit_character = DSW_TFR_KOS_mr_domino_char
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_character = DSW_TFR_KOS_mr_domino_char
					}
				}
				recruit_character = DSW_TFR_KOS_mr_domino_char
			}
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = ROOT
				character = DSW_TFR_KOS_mr_domino_char
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = DSW_TFR_KOS_mr_domino_char
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = Nat_Populism
				traits = {

				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = {
				DSW_FRA_party_leader_20 = token:DSW_TFR_KOS_mr_domino_char
			}
			DSW_FRA_refresh_party_leader = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = france.998.a
	}
}

	id = france.2

unit_leader_event = {
	id = france.153
	title = france.153.t
	desc = france.153.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_mussolini
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = france.153.a
		set_temp_variable = {
			party_index = 22
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			party_popularity_increase = -0.025
		}
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
		add_political_power = 25
		if = {
			limit = {
				THIS = {
					has_trait = trait_FRA_Happy_with_Regime
				}
			}
			add_war_support = 0.05
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				THIS = {
					has_trait = trait_FRA_Neutral_with_Regime
				}
			}
			THIS = {
				remove_unit_leader_trait = trait_FRA_Neutral_with_Regime
				add_unit_leader_trait = trait_FRA_Happy_with_Regime
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = happy_generals
				value = 1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = neutral_generals
				value = -1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = angered_generals
				value = 0
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = retired_generals
				value = 0
			}
			FRA_clamp_generals_var = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				THIS = {
					has_trait = trait_FRA_Disgusted_with_Regime
				}
			}
			THIS = {
				remove_unit_leader_trait = trait_FRA_Disgusted_with_Regime
				add_unit_leader_trait = trait_FRA_Neutral_with_Regime
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = happy_generals
				value = 0
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = neutral_generals
				value = 1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = angered_generals
				value = -1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = retired_generals
				value = 0
			}
			FRA_clamp_generals_var = yes
		}
	}

country_event = {
	trigger = {
		FRA = {
			has_country_flag = DSW_FRA_tfr_active
		}
	}
	id = france.166
	title = france.166.t
	desc = france.166.d
	picture = GFX_FPR_Revolutionary_Congress
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = democratic
				long_name = FRA_France_Unmoving_long
				name = FRA_France_Unmoving
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = neutrality
				long_name = FRA_Libertarian_Communist_Union_long
				name = FRA_Libertarian_Communist_Union
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = neutrality
				long_name = FRA_Workers_Struggle_long
				name = FRA_Workers_Struggle
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				coalition_partner_var_temp = 18
			}
			DSW_FRA_dep_remove_from_coalition = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				coalition_partner_var_temp = 19
			}
			DSW_FRA_dep_remove_from_coalition = yes
			set_temp_variable = {
				coalition_partner_var_temp = 3
			}
			DSW_FRA_dep_remove_from_coalition = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = france.166.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_power_balance_in_range = {
					id = FPR_Revolutionary_Congress
					range < FPR_Communist_alligned_range
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = FPR_anarchist_revolt_flag
			}
		}
		set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Revolutionary_French_State
		set_country_flag = FPR_Jacobin_path_flag
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		DSW_FRA_dep_change_government_type_communist_party_state = yes
		set_variable = {
			ruling_party = 4
		}
		set_variable = {
			DSW_FRA_tfr_party = 4
		}
		set_variable = {
			DSW_FRA_scripted_elections = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			disable_elections = 1
		}
		clear_array = gov_coalition_array
		set_country_flag = DSW_FRA_tfr_active
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = communism
			elections_allowed = no
			last_election = "2022.04.10"
			election_frequency = 60
			long_name = FRA_Patriots_of_the_Revolution_long
			name = FRA_Patriots_of_the_Revolution
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			DSW_FRA_refresh_party_leader = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			party_index = 4
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			party_popularity_increase = 0.08
		}
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		recalculate_party = yes
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = DSW_TFR_FPR_Antoine_Leaument_char
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = communist_state
				traits = {
					hog_pretty_boy_good
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = {
				DSW_FRA_party_leader_4 = token:DSW_TFR_FPR_Antoine_Leaument_char
			}
			DSW_FRA_refresh_party_leader = yes
		}
	}
}

					}

						x = 0
						y = 0
					}
				}

		}

				division = "name = \"Military Defence Force\" division_template = \"Military Defence Force\" start_experience_factor = 0.4 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
				owner = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
